name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_7997_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDoesn’t the world have enough desks and chairs to pick from? So, why do so many people think we need something new?\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBefore we decided that we needed something new, we studied the past. In today's world many people assume that all desks have always been flat tables, forever. It turns out that desks for writing and creating were seldom “flat”.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eEarly “writing” desks were sloped for ergonomic reasons. Drafting tables that allowed a person to tilt the desk to almost any angle and stand or sit while working. These were all commonly used until we moved to computers for these jobs.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhy do we put computers on flat tables? It is the invention of the mechanical typewriter and calculator in the mid-1800s that creates the flat topped desk in the office. While our current computers are vastly more sophisticated, they evolved from these machines, but the desks itself did not.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.com/p/images/attachments/original/000/066/928/66928-1654718877-bde78cacf628c3c511c766d4e1cf8aa54724b51f.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\" style=\"width: 405px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDecades ago this mattered less than it does today. People did not spend the time in front of a typewriter like they do a computer. In today’s world we are expected to bend our bodies over a computer from early schooling on. It is no wonder so many people are hurting.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAltwork is here to change that! We believe it’s time for a NEW way to work.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 s-paddingHoriz1 s-borderTop1 Solving difficult problems with innovative solutions, but then John was rear-ended on the freeway. It was a forceful crash, a life changing crash. His back was seriously injured and some of the treatment options could have resulted in paralysis. Being the kind of person John is, he started thinking about how to find\"MemorialTV\" a solution to his problem. Later, he approached Che and they started working together to resolve the issues that come with traditional tables and chairs.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhy do we think this is important? Our goal at Altwork is to enable people to be more comfortable and focused without the pain and unhealthiness of the traditional desk.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe know a workstation won’t
